AnimatedHeading-module-scss-module__KoWePa__heading AnimatedHeading-module-scss-module__KoWePa__revealed Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__heading" style="--word-stagger:0s"><span aria-hidden="true" class="AnimatedHeading-module-scss-module__KoWePa__word" style="--word-index:0">FAQ</span></h2></div><div class="PricingTabs-module-scss-module__6nmKJG__wrapper" data-cta-ignore=""><div class="PricingTabs-module-scss-module__6nmKJG__track"><div class="PricingTabs-module-scss-module__6nmKJG__tablist" role="tablist"><button aria-controls="pricing-faq-panel-0" aria-selected="true" class="PricingTabs-module-scss-module__6nmKJG__tab text-caption" id="pricing-faq-tab-0" role="tab" style="" tabindex="0" type="button">Plans and usage</button><button aria-controls="pricing-faq-panel-1" aria-selected="false" class="PricingTabs-module-scss-module__6nmKJG__tab text-caption" id="pricing-faq-tab-1" role="tab" style="" tabindex="-1" type="button">Billing and payments</button><button aria-controls="pricing-faq-panel-2" aria-selected="false" class="PricingTabs-module-scss-module__6nmKJG__tab text-caption" id="pricing-faq-tab-2" role="tab" style="" tabindex="-1" type="button">Managing your plan</button></div></div></div><div aria-labelledby="pricing-faq-tab-0" class="shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__raise" id="pricing-faq-panel-0" role="tabpanel"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__list"><div><h3 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__groupTitle text-headline-4-serif">Plans and usage</h3><div><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__item"><h4 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__heading4 shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__noTrim"><button aria-controls="_R_6cjlinpfivalb_-content" aria-expanded="false" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__trigger" data-cta-ignore="" id="_R_6cjlinpfivalb_-trigger" type="button"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__question shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__trim text-headline-6-serif">What do I get with Claude Pro?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__icon"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine"></span><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLineVertical"></span></span></button></h4><div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="_R_6cjlinpfivalb_-trigger" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__content" id="_R_6cjlinpfivalb_-content" inert="" role="region"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__contentInner"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__body text-body-2"><p>The <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/8325606-what-is-the-pro-plan" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Pro plan</a> gives you everything in a Free plan with more usage and more of Claude's capabilities. That includes Claude Code, along with projects to organize your chats and documents, access to more Claude models, and Claude for Microsoft 365. You can choose a monthly or annual subscription.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__item"><h4 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__heading4 shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__noTrim"><button aria-controls="_R_acjlinpfivalb_-content" aria-expanded="false" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__trigger" data-cta-ignore="" id="_R_acjlinpfivalb_-trigger" type="button"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__question shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__trim text-headline-6-serif">How are Max and Ultra different from Pro?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__icon"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine"></span><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLineVertical"></span></span></button></h4><div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="_R_acjlinpfivalb_-trigger" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__content" id="_R_acjlinpfivalb_-content" inert="" role="region"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__contentInner"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__body text-body-2"><p><a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/11049741-what-is-the-max-plan" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Max and Ultra</a> are built for people who work with Claude throughout the day and want longer, uninterrupted sessions. You get everything in Pro, plus more usage. Max gives you 5x the usage of Pro, every 5 hours and every week. Ultra gives you 10x the weekly usage of Pro, with no 5-hour limit for <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/17728952" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">verified accounts</a>. Both plans include Fable, our top model, for up to half your weekly limit, and a monthly API credit to build on the Claude Platform: $100 on Max, $200 on Ultra. Both plans are billed monthly. See the cards above for current pricing.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__item"><h4 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__heading4 shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__noTrim"><button aria-controls="_R_ecjlinpfivalb_-content" aria-expanded="false" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__trigger" data-cta-ignore="" id="_R_ecjlinpfivalb_-trigger" type="button"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__question shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__trim text-headline-6-serif">What are the usage limits on each plan, and what happens when I hit them?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__icon"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine"></span><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLineVertical"></span></span></button></h4><div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="_R_ecjlinpfivalb_-trigger" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__content" id="_R_ecjlinpfivalb_-content" inert="" role="region"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__contentInner"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__body text-body-2"><p>Every plan has <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/11647753-how-do-usage-and-length-limits-work" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">usage limits</a>. Most accounts have a limit that resets on a rolling five-hour session window, and paid plans add weekly limits on top. Your activity across Claude on web, desktop, mobile, and Claude Code all draws from the same pool. How much you can do depends on the length and complexity of your conversations, the model you choose, and the features you use, so there's no fixed message count. Free covers everyday questions. Pro gives you at least 5x more usage per 5-hour session than Free. Max gives you 5x the usage of Pro every 5 hours and every week. Ultra gives you 10x the weekly usage of Pro, with no 5-hour limit for <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/17728952" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">verified accounts</a>. On Team plans, Standard seats give more than Pro and Premium seats give 5x more than Standard. To manage capacity and make sure all users have fair access, we may limit your usage in other ways, such as weekly and monthly caps or model and feature usage, at our discretion. When you reach a limit, you can wait for it to reset, move to a higher plan, or, on paid plans, turn on <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/12429409-manage-usage-credits-for-paid-claude-plans">usage credits</a> to keep working at standard API rates. You can see where you stand anytime in <a href="https://claude.ai/redirect/claudedotcom.v1.bf398c81-ec6b-4136-871f-7d0bd7044e7c/settings/usage" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Settings &gt; Usage</a>.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__item"><h4 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__heading4 shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__noTrim"><button aria-controls="_R_icjlinpfivalb_-content" aria-expanded="false" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__trigger" data-cta-ignore="" id="_R_icjlinpfivalb_-trigger" type="button"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__question shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__trim text-headline-6-serif">Is Claude Code included in my plan?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__icon"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine"></span><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLineVertical"></span></span></button></h4><div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="_R_icjlinpfivalb_-trigger" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__content" id="_R_icjlinpfivalb_-content" inert="" role="region"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__contentInner"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__body text-body-2"><p><a href="https://claude.com/product/claude-code" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Claude Code</a> is included in all paid plans. It shares the same usage limits as the rest of your plan, so your work in the terminal and your chats draw from one pool. For heavy coding sessions, you can also switch to pay-as-you-go API credits through a <a href="https://platform.claude.com/" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Console account</a>.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__item"><h4 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__heading4 shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__noTrim"><button aria-controls="_R_mcjlinpfivalb_-content" aria-expanded="false" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__trigger" data-cta-ignore="" id="_R_mcjlinpfivalb_-trigger" type="button"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__question shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__trim text-headline-6-serif">How much does the Claude Enterprise plan cost?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__icon"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine"></span><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLineVertical"></span></span></button></h4><div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="_R_mcjlinpfivalb_-trigger" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__content" id="_R_mcjlinpfivalb_-content" inert="" role="region"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__contentInner"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__body text-body-2"><p>The <a href="https://claude.com/solutions/enterprise">Claude Enterprise plan</a> is built for large businesses operating at scale. Pricing is $20 per seat per month plus usage billed at API rates, so cost scales with the models and tasks your team runs. Enterprise is billed annually and includes everything in Team plus advanced security and admin controls, including SCIM, audit logs, custom data retention, and role-based access. You can get started self-serve or <a href="https://claude.com/contact-sales">contact sales</a> for a tailored quote.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div aria-labelledby="pricing-faq-tab-1" class="shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__raise" hidden="" id="pricing-faq-panel-1" role="tabpanel"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__list"><div><h3 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__groupTitle text-headline-4-serif">Billing and payments</h3><div><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__item"><h4 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__heading4 shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__noTrim"><button aria-controls="_R_6kjlinpfivalb_-content" aria-expanded="false" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__trigger" data-cta-ignore="" id="_R_6kjlinpfivalb_-trigger" type="button"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__question shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__trim text-headline-6-serif">Can I pay annually? Does it cost less than if I pay monthly?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__icon"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine"></span><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLineVertical"></span></span></button></h4><div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="_R_6kjlinpfivalb_-trigger" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__content" id="_R_6kjlinpfivalb_-content" inert="" role="region"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__contentInner"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__body text-body-2"><p>Yes, annual plans are designed to cost less than monthly subscriptions. Annual subscriptions are available for Pro and Team plans. Enterprise plans are only available as annual subscriptions.</p><p>When you choose to pay annually, you're billed once up front for the year. If you're already on a monthly plan, you can switch to annual in <a href="https://claude.ai/redirect/claudedotcom.v1.bf398c81-ec6b-4136-871f-7d0bd7044e7c/settings/billing" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Settings &gt; Billing</a>, and any unused time is credited toward your first annual invoice. See the steps for <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/10185996-how-to-change-your-pro-plan-from-monthly-to-annual-billing" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Pro</a> and <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/12083917-change-your-team-plan-from-monthly-to-annual-billing" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Team</a>.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__item"><h4 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__heading4 shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__noTrim"><button aria-controls="_R_akjlinpfivalb_-content" aria-expanded="false" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__trigger" data-cta-ignore="" id="_R_akjlinpfivalb_-trigger" type="button"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__question shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__trim text-headline-6-serif">Does Claude offer refunds?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__icon"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine"></span><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLineVertical"></span></span></button></h4><div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="_R_akjlinpfivalb_-trigger" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__content" id="_R_akjlinpfivalb_-content" inert="" role="region"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__contentInner"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__body text-body-2"><p>Payments are generally non-refundable, except where our <a href="https://www.anthropic.com/legal/consumer-terms" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Consumer Terms of Service</a> say otherwise or where local law requires it.</p><p>Customers outside of Europe can follow this process to <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/12386328-requesting-a-refund-for-a-paid-claude-plan" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">request a refund</a>:</p><ul><li>Log in, open Get help from the menu, start a message, and choose Claude Refund Request to check your eligibility.</li><li>If you subscribed through the App Store, <a href="https://support.apple.com/billing" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Apple</a> handles the refund, so you'll request it from Apple.</li><li>If you subscribed through Google Play, <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/9015913-how-to-get-support" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">contact our Support team</a> to check eligibility for an active subscription.</li></ul><p>If you're in the European Economic Area and the United Kingdom, and within your 14-day withdrawal period, you can request a refund directly in the app.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div aria-labelledby="pricing-faq-tab-2" class="shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__raise" hidden="" id="pricing-faq-panel-2" role="tabpanel"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__list"><div><h3 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__groupTitle text-headline-4-serif">Managing your plan</h3><div><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__item"><h4 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__heading4 shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__noTrim"><button aria-controls="_R_6sjlinpfivalb_-content" aria-expanded="false" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__trigger" data-cta-ignore="" id="_R_6sjlinpfivalb_-trigger" type="button"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__question shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__trim text-headline-6-serif">How do I upgrade, downgrade, or switch between plans?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__icon"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine"></span><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLineVertical"></span></span></button></h4><div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="_R_6sjlinpfivalb_-trigger" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__content" id="_R_6sjlinpfivalb_-content" inert="" role="region"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__contentInner"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__body text-body-2"><p>You can change your plan any time in Settings.</p><ul><li>For individual plans, go to <a href="https://claude.ai/redirect/claudedotcom.v1.bf398c81-ec6b-4136-871f-7d0bd7044e7c/settings/billing" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Settings &gt; Billing</a> and choose Manage subscription.</li><li>On Team plans, an Owner or Primary Owner manages this under Organization settings &gt; Billing. <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/12004354-purchase-and-manage-seats-on-team-plans" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">See more details here</a>.</li></ul><p>Upgrades take effect right away, and any unused time on your current plan is credited toward the new one, so you won't pay twice for the same days.</p><p>Moving from monthly to annual works the same way, with your remaining time credited to the first annual invoice.</p><p>In some upgrades (for example Pro Annual to Max Monthly), your remaining value on your old plan is larger than the cost of one billing period of the new plan. In this case, the excess will be stored as credit, which will automatically get used for future purchases.</p><p>Downgrades take effect at the end of your current billing period, and your chats, projects, and files stay with your account whichever plan you're on.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__item"><h4 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__heading4 shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__noTrim"><button aria-controls="_R_asjlinpfivalb_-content" aria-expanded="false" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__trigger" data-cta-ignore="" id="_R_asjlinpfivalb_-trigger" type="button"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__question shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__trim text-headline-6-serif">How do I cancel my Claude subscription?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__icon"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine"></span><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLineVertical"></span></span></button></h4><div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="_R_asjlinpfivalb_-trigger" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__content" id="_R_asjlinpfivalb_-content" inert="" role="region"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__contentInner"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__body text-body-2"><p>You can cancel anytime, and your plan stays active until the end of your current billing period. To avoid the next charge, cancel at least 24 hours before your renewal date.</p><ul><li>If you signed up on the web or the desktop app, go to <a href="https://claude.ai/redirect/claudedotcom.v1.bf398c81-ec6b-4136-871f-7d0bd7044e7c/settings/billing" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Settings &gt; Billing</a> and click Cancel.</li><li>If you subscribed through the App Store or Google Play, cancel from that app under Billing &gt; Manage subscription, or directly in your <a href="https://support.apple.com/en-us/118428" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Apple</a> or <a href="https://support.google.com/googleplay/answer/7018481" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Google Play</a> account.</li><li>Team and self-serve Enterprise: an Owner or Primary Owner cancels under <a href="https://claude.ai/redirect/claudedotcom.v1.bf398c81-ec6b-4136-871f-7d0bd7044e7c/admin-settings/billing" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Organization settings &gt; Billing</a>.</li><li>Sales-assisted Enterprise: Reach out to your Anthropic Contact or our Sales team to discuss cancellation.</li></ul><p>Canceling doesn't delete your data. Your chats, projects, and files stay with your account, though some features aren't available on the Free plan. For Pro, Max, and Ultra, your account moves to the Free plan once the period ends. For step-by-step help, see <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/8325617-how-do-i-cancel-my-paid-claude-subscription" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">how to cancel a Pro, Max, or Ultra plan</a> or <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/9267323-cancel-your-organization-s-team-plan-subscription">cancel a Team plan</a>.</p></div></div></div></div><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__item"><h4 class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__heading4 shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__noTrim"><button aria-controls="_R_esjlinpfivalb_-content" aria-expanded="false" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__trigger" data-cta-ignore="" id="_R_esjlinpfivalb_-trigger" type="button"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__question shared-module-scss-module__nl-56a__trim text-headline-6-serif">Does Claude offer volume discounts?</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__icon"><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine"></span><span class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLine Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__iconLineVertical"></span></span></button></h4><div aria-hidden="true" aria-labelledby="_R_esjlinpfivalb_-trigger" class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__content" id="_R_esjlinpfivalb_-content" inert="" role="region"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__contentInner"><div class="Faq-module-scss-module__DwF20q__body text-body-2"><p>Yes, Claude Enterprise and Claude API pricing can be customized for your organization with volume and committed-spend discounts through our sales team. We don't publish discount rates, so figures you see elsewhere may not reflect our pricing. <a href="https://claude.com/contact-sales" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">Contact sales</a> to learn more.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><div aria-hidden="true" style="display:none"></div></main></div></div><next-route-announcer style="position: absolute;"></next-route-announcer></body></html>
